Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..7)
find y: int(0..3)
find z: int(0..4)

such that

!(x % y % z = 3)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#rank_1: bool
  find x#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#rank_2),and([!(x#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#rank_1: bool
  find y#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(y#rank_2),or([!(y#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#rank_1: bool
  find z#rank_2: bool
  find z#rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#rank_3),and([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

!(x % y % z = 3),
or([!(x#rank_2),and([!(x#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),
true,
or([!(z#rank_3),and([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
!(x % y % z = 3),
true,
or([!(z#rank_3),and([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(or([!(x#rank_2),and([!(x#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

!(x % y % z = 3),
true,
or([!(z#rank_3),and([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
!(x % y % z = 3),
true
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(or([!(z#rank_3),and([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

x
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(5, 7)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2;int(1..)] [5, 7])

--

y
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 3)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2;int(1..)] [0, 3])

--

z
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4])

--

SATInt(Rank([(0, 3)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2;int(1..)] [0, 3]) % SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4])
   ~~> mod_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
{SafeMod(SATInt(Rank([(0, 3)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2;int(1..)] [0, 3]),SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4])) @ SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4]) != 0}

--

SATInt(Rank([(0, 4)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 4]) != 0
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
